# 3x3 piston door leaderboards

Generated by `mctas door leaderboard`; never edit it by hand. Every design on main and on the `explore/*` branches, judged once by main's verifier (3 open/close cycles; new designs at 32 positions, older verdicts at up to 32). Each board shows its leader (GIF from `door gallery`, video, how it works; a door leading several boards is shown once); click "more" for the rest. What every board means and the rules: [docs/rules.md](../../docs/rules.md). Every archived design with its video: [archive/README.md](archive/README.md). The story: [docs/history.md](../../docs/history.md).

**Size = volume**, as the piston door community counts a door's blocks (its regulation, section 4.2): the bounding box of the build over every state of a cycle (at rest, every tick of the opening and the closing, open), the lever (the input device) excluded. A door listed as `80b (8x10x1)` fits in 8 wide x 10 high x 1 deep, however many blocks it places. Every size board ranks by it, then by blocks placed (non-air blocks, piston heads excluded; the `blocks` column), then by open + close ticks. Measured once per design (`door properties`, cached in `archive/properties-v1.tsv`; definition: [tracks/props/NOTES.md](tracks/props/NOTES.md)). Archive file names keep the placed-block count they were archived with (`fc2-40b`): they are IDs, not sizes.

Walkway "clean" = nothing in the 3-deep walkway at rest (designs judged before the 2026-10-18 16:40Z "nothing in front of or behind the door" rule keep their verdicts). The boards rank rotation-safe designs whose 12 frame cells all hold the door block when open (FULL FRAME, vjeux's rule); the others are on [Not rotation-safe](#not-rotation-safe) and [Frame not full when open](#frame-not-full-when-open).

The whole solid record progression by blocks placed in one video (151 -> 40 blocks placed, `mctas door montage`; click the poster):

[![every solid record](https://github.com/vjeux/minecraft-tas/releases/download/videos/solid-progression.jpg)](https://github.com/vjeux/minecraft-tas/releases/download/videos/solid-progression.mp4)

The night, board by board (records by blocks placed, from the archive):

![records over the night](https://github.com/vjeux/minecraft-tas/releases/download/videos/night-timeline.png)

## Solid (main ranking)

9 identical plain door blocks, no sculk sensors; rotation-safe; full frame when open.

**56b** (volume 7x8x1, 41 blocks placed), solid, slime-free, flat, opens in 36 ticks, closes in 19 (worst cycle): [e78k-h5-41b](archive/e78k-h5-41b.txt). Agent e78k: e78k-h4-42b with a new bottom-right (ideal-rs --vary): the BR watcher (4,-1) QC-triggers a dropper (4,-3) through the air at (4,-2); an observer (3,-3) facing east on it fires into a dropper (2,-3) (powers B' = B1; the gate (2,-2) sees its triggered state = A2) and an observer (3,-2) facing down on (3,-3) fires K; the dust and the target are gone. 56b (7x8x1), 41 blocks, SOLID, slime-free, stone door, FULL seamless, rotation-safe, open 36 / close 19.

## Entities allowed

solid doors that may also use entities (minecarts, armor stands, boats...; category `entity`). Entities are counted apart from blocks: ranked by size (the volume), then entities, then blocks placed. Every entity must be back exactly where it rested, at rest, after each close, and nothing else may appear; every other board bans entities. Solid designs compete here too (0 entities); rotation-safe; full frame when open.

Same leader as [Solid (main ranking)](#solid-main-ranking): **56b** (volume 7x8x1, 41 blocks placed), opens in 36 ticks, closes in 19, [e78k-h5-41b](archive/e78k-h5-41b.txt).

## Seamless

solid doors with a SEAMLESS tier of the community regulation (QUART: nothing visible on the front side when closed; SEMI: also nothing in the hallway when open; FULL: also clean from the back when closed; SUPER: also clean at every tick while moving), ranked by tier, then whether the hallway can be built around it, then size (the volume), then blocks placed; rotation-safe.

![satwire-63b-49-49b](https://github.com/vjeux/minecraft-tas/releases/download/videos/satwire-63b-49-49b.gif)

**63b** (volume 7x9x1, 49 blocks placed), solid, slime-free, flat, opens in 15 ticks, closes in 15 (worst cycle): [satwire-63b-49-49b](archive/satwire-63b-49-49b.txt) ([video](https://github.com/vjeux/minecraft-tas/releases/download/videos/satwire-63b-49-49b.mp4)). ## Record-eligible (community regulation)

doors that meet the regulation's record eligibility (section 3): a SEAMLESS tier, the hallway can be built around them, the lever on the surface with steady support, works with repeater input too, unlimited uses, footsteps in the hallway harmless; ranked by size (the volume), then blocks placed; rotation-safe.

Same leader as [Seamless](#seamless): **63b** (volume 7x9x1, 49 blocks placed), opens in 15 ticks, closes in 15, [satwire-63b-49-49b](archive/satwire-63b-49-49b.txt).

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 63b (7x9x1) | 49 | FULL | 15 / 15 | `HEAD:designs/piston-door-3x3/archive/satwire-63b-49-49b.txt` |
